(Published by Authority)

PART IV (B) – LOCAL GOVERNMENT

Notices under the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance

NOTICE OF POLL

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE KEGALLE MUNICIPAL COUNCIL

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party and the Independent Group, the distinguishing number allotted to the independent group; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the Schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

K. H. M. Sൺඃංඍඁ P. Kൺඌඍඁඎඋංඌංඇ඀ඁൾ, Returning Oﬃ cer, Kegalle Municipal Council, Assistant Returning Oﬃ cer,Kegalle Administrative District, for Returning Oﬃ cer.Kegalle District Election Oﬃ ce,21st March, 2025.

NOTICE OF POLL

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE WARAKAPOLA PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

K. H. M. Sൺඃංඍඁ P. Kൺඌඍඁඎඋංඌංඇ඀ඁൾ, Returning Oﬃ cer, Warakapola Pradeshiya Sabha, Assistant Returning Oﬃ cer,Kegalle Administrative District, for Returning Oﬃ cer.

Kegalle District Election Oﬃ ce,21st March, 2025.

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE GALIGAMUWA PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

NOTICE OF POLL

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE KEGALLE PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party and the Independent Group, the distinguishing number allotted to the independent group; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party and the Independent Group, the distinguishing number allotted to the independent group; and

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE MAWANELLA PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party and the Independent Group, the distinguishing number allotted to the independent group; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling District of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE ARANAYAKE PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE YATIYANTOTA PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party and the Independent Group, the distinguishing number allotted to the independent group; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE BULATHKOHUPITIYA PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party and the Independent Group, the distinguishing number allotted to the independent group; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE RUWANWELLA PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party and the Independent Group, the distinguishing number allotted to the independent group; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE DERANIYAGALA PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.

NOTICE OF POLL

Local Authorities Elections Ordinance (Chapter 262)

Notice under Section 38(1)

ELECTION OF MEMBERS OF THE DEHIOWITA PRADESHIYA SABHA

NOTICE is hereby given under Section 38(1) of the Local Authorities Elections Ordinance that the above election is contested and that a poll will be taken between 7.00 a. m. and 4.00 p. m. on 06th May, 2025 and that :-

(a) the names of the candidates in the ﬁ rst nomination paper as indicated by the candidates of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group for Election as Members of the above mentioned Local Authority, the number and name of the respective wards and the names of candidates in the second nomination paper to be returned; and the approved election symbol allotted to each party and the Independent Group, the distinguishing number allotted to the independent group; and

(b) the situation of the polling station or stations and the particular polling stations reserved for female voters in the polling district of the above mentioned electoral area,

are speciﬁ ed in the schedule below.

2. The order in which the name of each Recognized Political Party and the Independent Group appearing in the Schedule below is the same order in which these particulars will be printed on the ballot paper.
